def _convolve_full(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n < 64: return np.convolve(a,a)
    N = _next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)[:2*n-1]
    # round small negatives
    if b.size: b = np.maximum(b,0)? But due numerical can be negative tiny. For max maybe not issue; use np.maximum(b,0) or no. Since a nonnegative, true nonnegative. But if previous tiny negative, max unaffected unless all negative no.
    return b
```
But for gradient weights top, we need q. Could use `np.fft.next_fast_len` from scipy.fft if available, else power of two.
```
def _next_fast_len(n):
    try:
        from scipy.fft import next_fast_len
        return next_fast_len(n)
    except Exception:
        # power of 2
        p=1
        while p<n: p*=2
        return p
```
But import inside top-level? OK.

Compute score quickly:
```
def _score(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return inf
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=1e-300: return inf
    b=_conv(a)
    maxb=b.max()
    return float(2*n*maxb/(S*S))
```
When normalize for optimization, S=1. But final evaluate uses same; returning sum=1.

def _optimize_smooth(a, start_beta, end_beta, deadline, eta_steps, momentum=0.9):
    a = normalize(a)
    best=a; best_score=score(a)
    beta = start_beta
    eta = 1e-3 / sqrt(n) maybe
    v = zeros
    no_improve=0
    while time<deadline:
        q = conv(a)
        qmax = q.max()
        cur=2*n*qmax
        if cur < best_score - 1e-12: update best; no_improve=0
        else no_improve+=1
        # compute weights
        z=(q-qmax)/beta
        # prevent under/over; qmax can be 0? no
        z=np.clip(z,-700,0)
        w=np.exp(z)
        # optionally top L? w full
        wsum=w.sum()
        if wsum<1e-300: # only max peak
            w[:] = 0; w[argmax]=1
        else w/=wsum
        grad = _grad_from_weights(w,a)*2? helper returns 2*
        grad_center = grad - grad.mean()
        # normalize direction
        gn = norm(grad_center)
        if gn<1e-15: break
        d = grad_center / gn
        # momentum
        v = momentum*v + (1-momentum)*d? Or heavy ball: v = momentum*v - d? Use direction = d + momentum*(d - prev_d)? Simpler: accumulate exponential moving: v = 0.9*v + d.
        # line search with decreasing trial steps: try eta, eta*0.5, etc. Use max exact score quickly.
        # To save time, maybe if no line search: a = project(a - eta*v/gn? )
        # We can accept if score improves; else reduce eta.
```

Detailed `_lbgfgs_smooth(x, beta)`:
- Input u length n. Compute m = max(u); y = exp(u-m); S=y.sum(); if S==0 return inf. a=y/S.
- q = _convolve_full(a); qmax=q.max()
- if qmax <=0: return 0, grad zero
- z = (q - qmax)/beta; clip -700,0
- e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); F = qmax + beta*log(s)
- w=e/s
- grad_a = _adjoint_weights(w,a) (returns 2*...)
- grad_u = a * (grad_a - np.dot(a, grad_a))
- return F, grad_u
Need gradient exact for Fβ? F depends on normalized a; grad_a is derivative wrt a on Euclidean (sum constraint?) If a normalized sum1; but grad_a computed without sum constraint. The derivative of F wrt a (with a variables, no sum constraint) is grad_a; however if we parameterize a=softmax(u), and F is scale-invariant? We defined F using normalized a=y/S. Since F(a) scale-invariant if a is normalized? Actually F(a)=β log∑exp(q(a)/β) where q scales as S^2 if a not normalized. Our computation normalizes a to sum1 inside. The objective is invariant to scaling of u? For a positive exact, Fnorm(a). We derive grad_u with Jacobian of normalized a. If we instead define function F_full(x) = F_scaled_softmax? Need consistent gradient. If we compute a=y/S and F(a), and grad_a = derivative of F w.r.t entries of normalized a subject to sum? But in softmax parameterization, a changes both due to y and S. The gradient of Fnorm w.r.t a (Euclidean, no constraint) can be computed (with no sum constraint) but Fnorm is not homogeneous? Let's implement `_core_smooth(x,beta)` top-level. It uses global `_N_VALUE`? It can infer n from len(x). To avoid repeated list allocation, use arrays.

Pseudo:
```
def _smooth_core(x, beta):
    n = len(x)
    u = x - np.mean(x)
    m = np.max(u)
    p = np.exp(u - m)
    s = np.sum(p)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       return 1e300, np.zeros(n)
    a = p/s
    # Ensure no negative; sum1
    q = _convolve_full(a)
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    if qmax <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n)
    if beta <= 0: beta=1e-12
    z = (q - qmax) / beta
    # clip z min -700, max 0
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    se = float(np.sum(e))
    if se <= 0 or not finite:
        w = np.zeros_like(q); imax=np.argmax(q); w[imax]=1.0; se=1.0
    else:
        w = e/se
    F = qmax + beta * np.log(se)
    grad_a = _grad_from_weights(w, a)  # already 2*?
    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_u = a * (grad_a - dot)
    # Clean nan
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_u)): grad_u=0
    return float(F), grad_u
```
`_grad_from_weights` using FFT. Need implement for any w length 2n-1 and a length n. But if w sparse full zeros? For full softmax maybe dense. Use FFT.

def _conv_with_fft(a):
    n=a.size; K=2*n-1; N=_fft_len(K)
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    q = np.fft.irfft(F*F, N)[:K].real

    return q, F, N
```

def _fft_len(m):
    if m in cache: return cache[m]
    try: from scipy.fft import next_fast_len; l=next_fast_len(m)
    except: power2
    cache[m]=l; return l

def _smooth_gradient(x, beta):
    if _LB_CACHE[0] is not None and _LB_CACHE[0] == beta:
        g = _LB_CACHE[2]
        _LB_CACHE[0]=None; _LB_CACHE[1]=None; _LB_CACHE[2]=None

```
def _conv_fft(a):
    n=len(a); K=2*n-1; N=_fft_len(K)
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    q = np.fft.irfft(F*F, N)[:K].real
    if q.size: np.maximum(q,0.0, out=q)
    return q, F, N
```
Then `_grad_from_weights_fft(w,a,F,N)`:
```

```

Can we compute r_fft without flipping by using phase from F_w? Could be: r = w[::-1] padded length K. DFT_r[m] = ∑_{i=0}^{K-1} w_{K-1-i} e^{-i2πmi/N} = e^{-i2πm(K-1)/N} ∑ w_j e^{i2πmj/N} = phase * conj(DFT_w[m]) if w real and padded? Same as before where flip within length K. Let j=K-1-i => i=K-1-j: =∑ w_j e^{-i2πm (K-1-j)/N} = e^{-i2πm(K-1)/N}∑ w_j e^{i2πm j/N} = phase*conj(DFT_w[m]) if w real. Yes. So we can compute `W = rfft(w,N); R = phase * np.conj(W)`. ```
_w_phase_cache = {}
def _grad_from_weights_fft(w,a,F,N,n):
    K=2*n-1
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)  # w padded
    # phase = exp(-2j*pi*m*(K-1)/N) for m=0..N//2
    ph = _get_phase(n,N)
    R = ph * np.conj(W)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * R, N)
    return 2.0 * c[n-1:K][::-1].real
```

def _subgradient_polish(a0, deadline, M=100, eta_init=None, beta_decay=0.0005):
   a=normalize floor
   best...
   n=len(a)
   eta=0.05/np.sqrt(n)
   v=zeros
   no=0
   beta_rel=0.001
   while time<deadline:
       q=conv; qmax; score
       update best; no
       # weights softmax over all q with beta_rel*qmax
       b = qmax*beta_rel
       w=softmax; grad=...
       grad_c = grad - np.mean(grad)
       gn=norm
       if gn small break
       # heavy ball
       v = 0.9*v + (1-0.9)*(grad_c/gn)
       # maybe step: a=project(a - eta*v,1)
       # line search: try eta, eta*0.5 twice; if none, keep and reduce eta
       acc=False
       for factor in [1,0.5,0.25,0.1]:
           trial = project(a - eta*factor*v, 1)
           s = score(trial)
           if s < score - 1e-15: a=trial; acc=True; break
       if not acc:
           eta *= 0.7
           no +=1
           if eta < 1e-8: break
       # anneal beta_rel slow
       beta_rel = max(1e-4, beta_rel*0.999)
       # if no improve many, reduce eta / reheat
```

def _adam_smooth(a0, beta, deadline, lr=0.2, b1=0.9, b2=0.999, print_every=...):
    n=len(a0); u=_a_to_u(a0); m1=zeros; m2=zeros; t=0; no=0; last_print=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       u = u - np.mean(u)
       F,g,a = _smooth_core(u,beta)
       _update_global(a)
       if F not finite or np.max(a)<... ?
       t+=1
       m1 = b1*m1 + (1-b1)*g
       m2 = b2*m2 + (1-b2)*(g*g)
       m1h = m1/(1-b1**t)
       m2h = m2/(1-b2**t)
       step = lr * m1h/(np.sqrt(m2h)+1e-8)
       u = u - step
       # center
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)): u=_a_to_u(a)
       # If no improve, reduce lr
       if t%?? evaluate exact? _update already.
       s=_GLOBAL_BEST_S[0]
       if s < previous_best - 1e-13: prev=s; lr=min(lr*1.02,2.0); no=0
       else: no+=1; if no%20==0: lr*=0.8; if lr<1e-4 break
    return _u_to_a(u)

`_power_core` details:
```
def _power_core(x, p, scale):
    a = _u_to_a(x)
    n=a.size
    q,Fa,N = _conv_fft(a)
    qmax = float(q.max())
    r = q / scale
    # for stability, r = np.clip(r, 0.0, 1e6)
    w = np.power(r, p-1.0)
    # To avoid overflow: if p-1 large and r large, use log? But r moderate. Use np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore')
    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if not finite or sw <=0:
       # fallback: weights on argmax
       w = np.zeros_like(q); imax=int(np.argmax(q)); w[imax]=1.0; sw=1.0
    F = (1.0 / p) * float(np.dot(w, q))  # consistent
    grad_a = _grad_from_weights_fft(w,a,Fa,N)
    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_u = a * (grad_a - dot)
    return F, grad_u, a, qmax
```

```
def _lbfgs_callback(xk):
    if time.monotonic() > _LB_DEADLINE[0]:
        raise _SearchTimeout()
    _LB_LAST_X[0] = np.array(xk, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
```

Define exception:
```
class _SearchTimeout(Exception):
    pass
```

Potential issue: Raising in callback may leave SciPy locks? okay.

Now, `_run_lbfgs_power`:
```
def _run_lbfgs_power(a0, p, scale, stage_deadline, maxiter=2000):
    if _scipy_minimize is None: return a0
    a0 = _normalize_floor(a0, 1e-10)
    u0 = _a_to_u(a0)
    _LB_CACHE[0]=None
    _LB_DEADLINE[0]=stage_deadline
    _LB_LAST_X[0]=None
    options = {'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': max(maxiter*5, 5000), 'maxls': 50, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'pgtol': 1e-10, 'disp': False, 'iprint': 0, 'm': 20}
    try:
       res = _scipy_minimize(_power_value, u0, jac=_power_gradient, args=(p,scale), method='L-BFGS-B', options=options, callback=_lbfgs_callback)
       x_opt = res.x if (res is not None and res.x is not None) else u0
    except _SearchTimeout:
       x_opt = _LB_LAST_X[0] if _LB_LAST_X[0] is not None else u0
    except Exception:
       x_opt = u0
    a = _u_to_a(x_opt) if x_opt is not None else a0
    return _normalize_floor(a,1e-12)
```

while time < deadline:
   F,g,a,qmax = _power_core(u,p,scale)
   _update_from_qmax(a,qmax)
   if score improved: lr_current = min(2.0, lr_current*1.05); no=0
   else: no+=1; if no>30: lr_current*=0.7; if lr_current<1e-4 break
   t+=1
   m1 = b1*m1 + (1-b1)*g
   m2 = b2*m2 + (1-b2)*(g*g)
   m1h = m1/(1-b1**t)
   m2h = m2/(1-b2**t)
   u = u - lr_current * m1h/(np.sqrt(m2h)+1e-12)
   u = u - np.mean(u)
   if not finite: break
```

`_grad_from_weights_fft`:
```
def _grad_from_weights_fft(w, a, F, N):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    K=2*n-1
    if n <= 32: # direct maybe faster and easier verify?
       grad=np.zeros(n)
       # Could use loops; but small n not main.
       for k,wk in enumerate(w):
           if wk ==0: continue
           lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
           # vector j
           j_range=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
           m_range=k - j_range
           grad[j_range] += 2.0*wk*a[m_range]
       return grad
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    ph = _get_phase(n,N)
    R = ph * np.conj(W)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * R, N)
    return 2.0 * c[n-1:K][::-1].real
```
For main n large, FFT. Direct small not needed but for fallback if N too small? Could just use FFT for all n; for n=1, N=1, arrays okay. `c[n-1:K]` = [0:1]; reverse. Good.

Need `np.conj(W)` each call; okay.

Now, `_conv_fft` for n small: Use np.convolve direct maybe faster. But for consistency use FFT. If n=1,K=1,N=1, rfft length1 works. q length1. Good.

Now, `_u_to_a`: If x length n. Need if x contains inf. Implement:
```
def _u_to_a(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    n=x.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
       x=np.zeros(n)
    x = x - np.mean(x)
    m = np.max(x)
    if not np.isfinite(m): m=0
    p = np.exp(x - m)
    s = np.sum(p)
    if s <= 0 or not finite:
       return np.ones(n)/n
    a = p/s
    return a
```
This always sum1. For x with huge range, underflow zeros; okay.

u=np.log(a)
return u - np.mean(u)
```

def _smoothed_root(n, k):

    kernel = np.ones(k); kernel/=k
    conv = np.convolve(r, kernel, mode='same')[:n]
    # Need ensure length n; same gives length 2n-1? For equal lengths, mode same returns length max(n,k)=n? np.convolve mode='same' returns length max(M,N)=n. Good.

```

`_smooth_perturb(a, rng, scale)`:
Generate low-freq noise as previous:
```
def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, scale=0.05):
    n=len(a); noise=np.zeros(n)
    nfreq = min(8, n//4)
    if nfreq < 1: nfreq=1
    for _ in range(nfreq):
       f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, nfreq+1))) # frequencies 1..nfreq
       phase = float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       amp = float(rng.uniform(-1,1))
       noise += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*f*np.arange(n)/n + phase)
    mx = np.max(np.abs(noise)); if mx>0: noise/=mx
    b = a + scale*noise
    return _normalize_floor(b, 1e-10)
```
Could also random coordinate transfer: pick few indices, move mass from peaks to valleys. But smooth enough.

Top:
```
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    from scipy.fft import next_fast_len as _next_fast_len
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None
    _next_fast_len = None

class _SearchTimeout(Exception):
    pass

_GLOBAL_BEST_A = [None]
_GLOBAL_BEST_S = [float('inf')]
_FFT_LEN_CACHE = {}
_PHASE_CACHE = {}
_LB_CACHE = [None, None, None]
_LB_DEADLINE = [0.0]
_LB_LAST_X = [None]
```

Helper:
```
def _fft_len(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1: return 1
    if m in _FFT_LEN_CACHE: return _FFT_LEN_CACHE[m]
    L = None
    if _next_fast_len is not None:
        try: L = int(_next_fast_len(m))
        except Exception: L=None
    if L is None or L < m:
        p=1
        while p < m: p <<= 1
        L=p
    _FFT_LEN_CACHE[m]=L
    return L
```
`next_fast_len(12241)` may return 12288; if at least m. Good.

`_conv_fft(a)`:
```
def _conv_fft(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0: return np.zeros(0), None, 1
    K = 2*n - 1
    N = _fft_len(K)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    q = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:K]
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    if q.size:
        np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
    return q, A, N
```
If q has tiny negative, max. If due numerical some q negative but all? max maybe 0 if all negative? no.

`_score(a)`:
```
def _score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    S=a.sum()
    if S <= 1e-300: return inf
    q,_,_ = _conv_fft(a)
    if q.size==0: return inf
    maxq=q.max()
    return float(2.0*n*maxq/(S*S))
```
Since we often pass normalized, this scales. If a has negative? we pass nonnegative; if negative, conv may have negative max; but false. We can clip: `a=np.maximum(a,0)` inside score? Evaluator clamps negative; for internal, do `a = np.maximum(a,0.0)`. Good.

`_normalize_floor`:
```
def _normalize_floor(a, floor=1e-12):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S <= 1e-300 or a.size ==0:
        return np.ones(max(1,a.size), dtype=np.float64)/max(1,a.size)
    a = a / S
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    S = a.sum()
    if S <= 1e-300: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
    return a / S
```
If a empty, returns length1? Better if empty return empty? But sequence must nonempty. For invalid, propose handles.

`_update_from_qmax`:
```
def _update_from_qmax(a, qmax):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n ==0 or not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax < 0: return
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(qmax)
    if not np.isfinite(score): return
    if score < _GLOBAL_BEST_S[0] - 1e-14:
        arr = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        S = arr.sum()
        if S <= 1e-300:
            arr = np.ones(n)/n
        else:
            arr = arr / S
        _GLOBAL_BEST_S[0] = score
        _GLOBAL_BEST_A[0] = arr.copy()
```
But if we normalize after clipping, score may change slightly if clipping changed sum. Clip no, normalizing no. If a already sum1. If floor? no. Good. If a has tiny negative clipped to 0, sum decreases maybe <1; normalize scale increases score? scale invariant if normalize? score invariant if normalize; okay. But score computed before clip/normalization; if clipping removed negative tiny, score same. If normalizing after sum not 1, score invariant. Good.

`_update_global(a, score=None)` uses `_score` if None.

`_u_to_a`, `_a_to_u` as above.

`_get_phase(n,N)`:
```
def _get_phase(n,N):
    key=(int(n), int(N))
    if key not in _PHASE_CACHE:
       m = np.arange(N//2 + 1, dtype=np.float64)
       K = 2*n - 1
       ph = np.exp((-2j * np.pi / float(N)) * m * float(K - 1))
       _PHASE_CACHE[key] = ph
    return _PHASE_CACHE[key]
```
For K-1=2n-2.

`_grad_from_weights_fft`:
```
def _grad_from_weights_fft(w, a, F, N):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n ==0: return np.zeros(0)
    K=2*n-1
    if n <= 24:
       grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
       for k in range(K):
          wk = float(w[k])
          if wk == 0.0: continue
          lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
          hi = min(n - 1, k)
          if lo <= hi:
             j = np.arange(lo, hi+1)
             m = k - j
             grad[j] += 2.0 * wk * a[m]
       return grad
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    ph = _get_phase(n,N)
    R = ph * np.conj(W)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * R, N)
    return 2.0 * c[n-1:K][::-1].real
```
For n=1,K=1,N=1, if n<=24 direct, fine. For main, FFT.

`_power_core`:
```
def _power_core(x, p, scale):
    a = _u_to_a(x)
    n = a.size
    if n ==0:
       return 1e300, np.zeros(0), a, 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(p) or p < 1.0: p=2.0
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300: scale = 1.0/float(n)
    q, F, N = _conv_fft(a)
    if q.size == 0: return 1e300, np.zeros(n), a, 0.0
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    r = q / scale
    # Protect overflow: if any r > 1e6, clip? use max
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(r)):
       r = np.zeros_like(q); imax=int(np.argmax(q)); r[imax]=1.0
    else:
       r = np.clip(r, 0.0, 1e6)
    exponent = float(p) - 1.0
    with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
       w = np.power(r, exponent)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
       w = np.nan_to_num(w, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
          w = np.zeros_like(q)
    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if sw <= 1e-300:
       w = np.zeros_like(q)
       imax = int(np.argmax(q))
       w[imax] = 1.0
    # F raw = (1/p) sum w q
    Fraw = (1.0/float(p)) * float(np.dot(w, q))
    mult = 1.0 / scale
    F = Fraw * mult
    if not np.isfinite(F): F = 1e300
    grad_a = _grad_from_weights_fft(w, a, F, N)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_a)):
        grad_a = np.zeros(n)
    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_u = a * (grad_a - dot)
    grad_u = grad_u * mult
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_u)):
        grad_u = np.zeros(n)
    return float(F), grad_u, a, qmax
```
Is F derivative with `w` after clipping? If clipping r at 1e6 non-smooth, but rare. `w` nan_to_num may change. Fine.

If p=2, exponent=1, w=r, Fraw=(1/2)∑r q = (1/(2scale))∑q^2. mult=1/scale -> F=∑q^2/(2scale^2). Derivative? F=1/(2s^2)∑q^2; dF/dq=1/s^2 q; true derivative =∑w 2a with w=q/s. Wait w*r? q/s = r. w = r. ∑w ∂q =∑(q/s)∂q =1/s ∂(∑q^2)/2 =1/s O. Our gradient from weights w gives 2∑(q/s)a = (1/s)2∑q a. F derivative: ∂q=2... so dF=1/s^2 ∑q 2... = (1/s)∑(q/s)2...? Yes (1/s^2)*q*2a = (1/s)(q/s)*2a. Our gradient uses weights w=q/s without extra 1/s? Wait `_grad_from_weights_fft` returns 2∑w a. Here w = r = q/s. That is ∂F/da? dF/da = (1/s^2)∑q*2a = (1/s)∑(q/s)2a = (1/s)*2∑r a. Our returned gradient lacks 1/s factor! But we then multiply grad_u by mult=1/scale, giving (1/s)*2∑r a. Good! F scaled by mult? F=mult*Fraw = 1/s * (1/p)∑ r^p? For p=2, Fraw=1/2∑r q=1/(2s)∑q^2; F=∑q^2/(2s^2). derivative =∑q/s *2a /s? Yes matches. Good.

For general p: Fraw=(1/p)∑r^p. F=Fraw/s. derivative wrt q = (1/s)*r^{p-1}. `_grad` returns 2∑r^{p-1}a; multiply mult=1/s => correct. Good.

Now, `_power_value` and gradient:
```
def _power_value(x, p, scale):
    F,g,a,qmax = _power_core(x,p,scale)
    _update_from_qmax(a,qmax)
    _LB_CACHE[0]=p; _LB_CACHE[1]=scale; _LB_CACHE[2]=g
    return float(F)

def _power_gradient(x, p, scale):
    if _LB_CACHE[0] is not None and _LB_CACHE[0] == p and _LB_CACHE[1] == scale:
        g = _LB_CACHE[2]
        _LB_CACHE[0]=None; _LB_CACHE[1]=None; _LB_CACHE[2]=None
        return g
    F,g,a,qmax = _power_core(x,p,scale)
    _update_from_qmax(a,qmax)
    return g
```
Need if p passed as int/float equality. Use `float` maybe. If `_LB_CACHE[0]` is None. Good.

It might overwrite? In L-BFGS-B, fprime returns array, C copies? Probably. To be safe but cost O(n) each iteration (small). Could return `g` directly. If it modifies, next? Since g only used once. def _lbfgs_callback(xk):
    if time.monotonic() > _LB_DEADLINE[0]:
        raise _SearchTimeout()
    try:
        _LB_LAST_X[0] = np.array(np.asarray(xk, dtype=np.float64), copy=True)

        pass

`_run_lbfgs_power`:
```
def _run_lbfgs_power(a0, p, scale, stage_deadline, maxiter=5000):
    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        return a0
    a0 = _normalize_floor(a0, 1e-10)
    u0 = _a_to_u(a0)
    _LB_CACHE[0]=None; _LB_CACHE[1]=None; _LB_CACHE[2]=None
    _LB_DEADLINE[0] = float(stage_deadline)
    _LB_LAST_X[0] = None
    maxiter = max(50, int(maxiter))
    options = {'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': max(50000, maxiter*10), 'maxls': 50, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'pgtol': 1e-10, 'disp': False, 'iprint': 0, 'm': 20}
    try:
       res = _scipy_minimize(_power_value, u0, jac=_power_gradient, args=(float(p), float(scale)), method='L-BFGS-B', options=options, callback=_lbfgs_callback)
       if res is not None and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
           x_opt = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
       else:
           x_opt = u0
    except _SearchTimeout:
       x_opt = _LB_LAST_X[0] if _LB_LAST_X[0] is not None else u0
    except Exception as e:
       print("L-BFGS stage failed:", repr(e))
       x_opt = u0
    a = _u_to_a(x_opt)
    return _normalize_floor(a, 1e-12)
```
`maxfun` big 50000 maybe too high but timeout. Good.

Now, root functions.
```
def _geometric_root(n):
    p=np.empty(n); c=1.0
    for k in range(n):
        p[k]=c
        if k+1 < n:
            c *= (2.0*k + 1.0) / (2.0*(k+1.0))
    return p
```
Check for k large: factor ~1 - 1/(2k), c decays. Good. Could underflow? n 6121, c ~0.013. fine.

`_sym_normalize(a)` and `_smooth_perturb`.

`_smooth_perturb` needs rng. Use `np.arange` each time; for n=6121 and few, fine.
```
def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, scale=0.05):
    n=len(a)
    noise=np.zeros(n)
    maxf = max(1, min(8, n//4))
    for _ in range(maxf):
       f = int(rng.integers(1, maxf+1))
       ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
       amp = float(rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0))
       noise += amp * np.cos((2.0*np.pi*f/n)*np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64) + ph)
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(noise)))
    if mx > 1e-300: noise /= mx
    b = a + scale*noise
    return _normalize_floor(b, 1e-10)
```
If noise all zero, returns a.

Maybe a random high-frequency perturbation? Smooth low-freq okay.

Variant generation:
```
def _root_variants(n):
    roots=[]
    r=_geometric_root(n)
    roots.append(_normalize_floor(r,1e-12))
    roots.append(_normalize_floor((r+r[::-1])*0.5,1e-12))
    for k in (3,5):
       kernel=np.ones(k)/k
       cv=np.convolve(r, kernel, mode='same')
       if cv.size > n: cv=cv[:n]
       elif cv.size < n: cv=np.concatenate([cv, np.zeros(n-cv.size)])
       roots.append(_normalize_floor(cv,1e-12))
    return roots
```
`np.convolve` with r length n, kernel k small returns length n+k-1 if mode full? mode='same' returns length max(n,k)=n? Let's confirm: numpy convolve mode='same' returns output of length max(M,N), centered. If M=n, N=k, output n. Good. No need adjust. But if n=1 maybe. Use try.

```python
"""
Search for low normalized autocorrelation-peak non-negative step sequences.

The optimizer minimizes
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
by working on the simplex sum(a)=1, a>=0.  A fast FFT-based smooth p-norm
relaxation of the maximum convolution coefficient is optimized with L-BFGS-B
on log-softmax coordinates, with annealed p and restarts.  A final Adam-style
polish is applied, followed by exact evaluation and comparison with the
provided parent construction.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    from scipy.fft import next_fast_len as _next_fast_len
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None
    _next_fast_len = None


class _SearchTimeout(Exception):
    pass


_GLOBAL_BEST_A = [None]
_GLOBAL_BEST_S = [float("inf")]
_FFT_LEN_CACHE = {}
_PHASE_CACHE = {}
_LB_CACHE = [None, None, None]
_LB_DEADLINE = [0.0]
_LB_LAST_X = [None]


def _fft_len(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if m in _FFT_LEN_CACHE:
        return _FFT_LEN_CACHE[m]
    out = None
    if _next_fast_len is not None:
        try:
            out = int(_next_fast_len(m))
        except Exception:
            out = None
    if out is None or out < m:
        p = 1
        while p < m:
            p <<= 1
        out = p
    _FFT_LEN_CACHE[m] = out
    return out


def _conv_fft(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), None, 1
    K = 2 * n - 1
    N = _fft_len(K)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    q = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:K]
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    if q.size:
        np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
    return q, A, N


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    q, _, _ = _conv_fft(a)
    if q.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(q)) / (S * S))


def _normalize_floor(a, floor=1e-12):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / S
    a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / S


def _update_global(a, score=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return
    if score is None:
        score = _score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    if score < _GLOBAL_BEST_S[0] - 1e-14:
        arr = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        S = float(np.sum(arr))
        if S <= 1e-300:
            arr = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            arr = arr / S
        _GLOBAL_BEST_S[0] = float(score)
        _GLOBAL_BEST_A[0] = arr.copy()


def _update_from_qmax(a, qmax):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0 or not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax < 0.0:
        return
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(qmax)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    if score < _GLOBAL_BEST_S[0] - 1e-14:
        arr = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        S = float(np.sum(arr))
        if S <= 1e-300:
            arr = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            arr = arr / S
        _GLOBAL_BEST_S[0] = float(score)
        _GLOBAL_BEST_A[0] = arr.copy()


def _u_to_a(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(x.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
    m = float(np.max(x))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        m = 0.0
    p = np.exp(x - m)
    S = float(np.sum(p))
    if S <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / S


def _a_to_u(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / S
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / S
    u = np.log(a)
    u = u - float(np.mean(u))
    return u


def _get_phase(n, N):
    key = (int(n), int(N))
    if key in _PHASE_CACHE:
        return _PHASE_CACHE[key]
    m = np.arange(N // 2 + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    K = 2 * n - 1
    ph = np.exp((-2.0j * np.pi / float(N)) * m * float(K - 1))
    _PHASE_CACHE[key] = ph
    return ph


def _grad_from_weights_fft(w, a, F, N):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    K = 2 * n - 1

    if n <= 24:
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(K):
            wk = float(w[k])
            if wk == 0.0:
                continue
            lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, k)
            if lo <= hi:
                j = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
                m = k - j
                grad[j] += 2.0 * wk * a[m]
        return grad

    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    ph = _get_phase(n, N)
    R = ph * np.conj(W)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * R, N)
    return 2.0 * c[n - 1:K][::-1].real


def _power_core(x, p, scale):
    a = _u_to_a(x)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e300, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), a, 0.0

    if not np.isfinite(p) or p < 1.0:
        p = 2.0
    p = float(p)
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
        scale = 1.0 / float(n)
    scale = float(scale)

    q, F, N = _conv_fft(a)
    if q.size == 0:
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), a, 0.0

    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    r = q / scale
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(r)):
        r = np.zeros_like(q)
        imax = int(np.argmax(q))
        r[imax] = 1.0
    else:
        r = np.clip(r, 0.0, 1.5)

    exponent = p - 1.0
    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        w = np.power(r, exponent)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        w = np.nan_to_num(w, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
            w = np.zeros_like(q)

    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if sw <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(q)
        imax = int(np.argmax(q))
        w[imax] = 1.0

    Fraw = (1.0 / p) * float(np.dot(w, q))
    mult = 1.0 / scale
    F = Fraw * mult
    if not np.isfinite(F):
        F = 1e300

    grad_a = _grad_from_weights_fft(w, a, F, N)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_a)):
        grad_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_u = a * (grad_a - dot)
    grad_u = grad_u * mult
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_u)):
        grad_u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    return float(F), grad_u, a, qmax


def _power_value(x, p, scale):
    F, g, a, qmax = _power_core(x, p, scale)
    _update_from_qmax(a, qmax)
    _LB_CACHE[0] = p
    _LB_CACHE[1] = scale
    _LB_CACHE[2] = g
    return float(F)


def _power_gradient(x, p, scale):
    if _LB_CACHE[0] is not None and _LB_CACHE[0] == p and _LB_CACHE[1] == scale:
        g = _LB_CACHE[2]
        _LB_CACHE[0] = None
        _LB_CACHE[1] = None
        _LB_CACHE[2] = None
        return g
    F, g, a, qmax = _power_core(x, p, scale)
    _update_from_qmax(a, qmax)
    return g


def _lbfgs_callback(xk):
    try:
        _LB_LAST_X[0] = np.array(np.asarray(xk, dtype=np.float64), copy=True)
    except Exception:
        pass
    if time.monotonic() > _LB_DEADLINE[0]:
        raise _SearchTimeout()


def _run_lbfgs_power(a0, p, scale, stage_deadline, maxiter=4000):
    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        return _normalize_floor(a0, 1e-12)

    a0 = _normalize_floor(a0, 1e-10)
    u0 = _a_to_u(a0)

    _LB_CACHE[0] = None
    _LB_CACHE[1] = None
    _LB_CACHE[2] = None
    _LB_DEADLINE[0] = float(stage_deadline)
    _LB_LAST_X[0] = None

    maxiter = max(50, int(maxiter))
    options = {
        "maxiter": maxiter,
        "maxfun": max(50000, maxiter * 10),
        "maxls": 50,
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "pgtol": 1e-10,
        "disp": False,
        "iprint": 0,
        "m": 20,
    }

    try:
        res = _scipy_minimize(
            _power_value,
            u0,
            jac=_power_gradient,
            args=(float(p), float(scale)),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options=options,
            callback=_lbfgs_callback,
        )
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            x_opt = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            x_opt = u0
    except _SearchTimeout:
        x_opt = _LB_LAST_X[0] if _LB_LAST_X[0] is not None else u0
    except Exception as exc:
        print("L-BFGS stage failed:", repr(exc))
        x_opt = u0

    a = _u_to_a(x_opt)
    return _normalize_floor(a, 1e-12)


def _adam_power(a0, p, scale, deadline, lr=0.18):
    a0 = _normalize_floor(a0, 1e-10)
    u = _a_to_u(a0)
    n = int(u.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a0

    m1 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    b1c = 1.0
    b2c = 1.0
    t = 0
    lr_cur = float(lr)
    prev_best = float(_GLOBAL_BEST_S[0])
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        F, g, a, qmax = _power_core(u, p, scale)
        _update_from_qmax(a, qmax)

        if _GLOBAL_BEST_S[0] < prev_best - 1e-12:
            prev_best = float(_GLOBAL_BEST_S[0])
            no_improve = 0
            lr_cur = min(2.0, lr_cur * 1.05)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 40:
                no_improve = 0
                lr_cur *= 0.72
                if lr_cur < 1e-4:
                    break

        t += 1
        b1c = max(b1c * (1.0 - b1), 1e-300)
        b2c = max(b2c * (1.0 - b2), 1e-300)

        m1 = b1 * m1 + (1.0 - b1) * g
        m2 = b2 * m2 + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)

        v1 = m1 / b1c
        v2 = m2 / b2c
        step = lr_cur * v1 / (np.sqrt(v2) + 1e-12)
        u = u - step

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
            u = _a_to_u(a)
        u = u - float(np.mean(u))

    F, g, a, qmax = _power_core(u, p, scale)
    _update_from_qmax(a, qmax)
    return _normalize_floor(a, 1e-12)


def _geometric_root(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = 1.0
    for k in range(n):
        p[k] = c
        if k + 1 < n:
            c *= (2.0 * k + 1.0) / (2.0 * (k + 1.0))
    return p


def _sym_normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    b = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
    return _normalize_floor(b, 1e-12)


def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, scale=0.05):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    maxf = max(1, min(8, n // 4))
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    for _ in range(maxf):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, maxf + 1))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
        noise += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + ph)
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(noise)))
    if mx > 1e-300:
        noise = noise / mx
    b = a + float(scale) * noise
    return _normalize_floor(b, 1e-10)


def _choose_best_of(cands):
    best = None
    bs = float("inf")
    for c in cands:
        arr = _normalize_floor(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64), 1e-12)
        if arr.size == 0:
            continue
        s = _score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < bs:
            bs = s
            best = arr
    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        bs = 2.0
    return best, float(bs)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0
    hard_deadline = t_start + max(0.0, budget - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        raw = None

    arr = None
    if raw is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            arr = None

    if arr is None or arr.size == 0:
        n = 6121
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        n = int(arr.size)

    arr = _normalize_floor(arr, 1e-12)
    parent_arr = arr.copy()

    _GLOBAL_BEST_A[0] = None
    _GLOBAL_BEST_S[0] = float("inf")
    _update_global(arr)

    parent_list = [float(x) for x in parent_arr]
    try:
        parent_exact = float(evaluate_sequence(parent_list))
    except Exception:
        parent_exact = float(_GLOBAL_BEST_S[0])

    root = _geometric_root(n)
    root_cands = [
        root,
        _sym_normalize(root),
    ]
    for kk in (3, 5):
        kernel = np.ones(kk, dtype=np.float64) / float(kk)
        cv = np.convolve(root, kernel, mode="same")
        cv = np.asarray(cv, dtype=np.float64)
        if cv.size > n:
            cv = cv[:n]
        elif cv.size < n:
            cv = np.concatenate([cv, np.zeros(n - cv.size, dtype=np.float64)])
        root_cands.append(cv)

    root_best, root_best_score = _choose_best_of(root_cands)

    cands = [arr, root_best]
    cands.append(_sym_normalize(arr))
    cands.append(_smooth_perturb(arr, rng, 0.03))
    cands.append(_smooth_perturb(arr, rng, 0.08))

    for c in cands:
        cc = _normalize_floor(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64), 1e-12)
        if cc.size == 0:
            continue
        _update_global(cc)

    if root_best is not None:
        _update_global(root_best, _score(root_best))

    print("initial best score: %.8f" % float(_GLOBAL_BEST_S[0]))

    used_lbfgs = False
    if _scipy_minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 120:
        used_lbfgs = True
        stage_defs = [
            (8, "best"),
            (16, "best"),
            (32, "best"),
            (64, "best"),
            (16, "perturb"),
            (32, "perturb"),
            (64, "perturb"),
            (16, "root"),
            (32, "root"),
            (64, "perturb2"),
        ]
        base_stage_time = 200.0
        last_best = float(_GLOBAL_BEST_S[0])
        no_improve = 0

        for idx, (p, typ) in enumerate(stage_defs):
            if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 150:
                break
            remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
            st = min(base_stage_time, max(30.0, remaining - 150.0))
            if st <= 10.0:
                break
            stage_start = time.monotonic()
            stage_deadline = stage_start + st

            best_a = _GLOBAL_BEST_A[0]
            if best_a is None:
                best_a = parent_arr
            start = best_a
            if typ == "perturb":
                start = _smooth_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.06)
            elif typ == "perturb2":
                start = _smooth_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.13)
            elif typ == "root":
                start = root_best

            s_start = _score(start)
            scale = max(s_start / (2.0 * n), 1e-12) * 1.02

            _run_lbfgs_power(start, p, scale, stage_deadline, maxiter=4000)

            stage_time = time.monotonic() - stage_start
            cur_best = float(_GLOBAL_BEST_S[0])
            print(
                "stage %d p=%d typ=%s elapsed=%.1fs best=%.8f"
                % (idx + 1, int(p), typ, stage_time, cur_best)
            )

            if cur_best < last_best - 1e-12:
                last_best = cur_best
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        while time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 150:
            p = 64 if rng.random() < 0.75 else 32
            sc = float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.14))
            best_a = _GLOBAL_BEST_A[0]
            if best_a is None:
                best_a = parent_arr
            start = _smooth_perturb(best_a, rng, sc)
            s_start = _score(start)
            scale = max(s_start / (2.0 * n), 1e-12) * 1.02
            remaining = hard_deadline - 150.0 - time.monotonic()
            st = min(120.0, max(20.0, remaining))
            if st < 10.0:
                break
            stage_start = time.monotonic()
            _run_lbfgs_power(start, p, scale, stage_start + st, maxiter=2500)
            print(
                "extra L-BFGS p=%d elapsed=%.1fs best=%.8f"
                % (int(p), time.monotonic() - stage_start, float(_GLOBAL_BEST_S[0]))
            )

    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 60 and _GLOBAL_BEST_A[0] is not None:
        total_adam = max(30.0, remaining - 40.0)
        if used_lbfgs:
            adam_ps = [16, 32, 64]
        else:
            adam_ps = [4, 8, 16, 32, 64]
        per = total_adam / max(1, len(adam_ps))
        for p in adam_ps:
            if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 30:
                break
            a_cur = _GLOBAL_BEST_A[0]
            if a_cur is None:
                a_cur = parent_arr
            s_cur = _score(a_cur)
            scale = max(s_cur / (2.0 * n), 1e-12) * 1.01
            dl = min(time.monotonic() + per, hard_deadline - 20.0)
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            _adam_power(a_cur, p, scale, dl, lr=0.18)
            print(
                "Adam p=%d elapsed=%.1fs best=%.8f"
                % (int(p), time.monotonic() - t0, float(_GLOBAL_BEST_S[0]))
            )

    final_candidates = [parent_arr, _GLOBAL_BEST_A[0], root_best]
    best_list = None
    best_exact = float("inf")

    seen = []
    for c in final_candidates:
        if c is None:
            continue
        cc = _normalize_floor(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64), 1e-12)
        if cc.size == 0:
            continue
        lst = [float(x) for x in cc]
        key = (len(lst), float(lst[0]) if len(lst) else 0.0, float(lst[-1]) if len(lst) else 0.0)
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.append(key)
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_list = lst

    if best_list is None:
        best_list = parent_list
        try:
            best_exact = float(evaluate_sequence(best_list))
        except Exception:
            best_exact = float(parent_exact)

    if parent_exact < best_exact:
        best_list = parent_list
        best_exact = parent_exact

    if not np.isfinite(best_exact):
        best_list = parent_list
        try:
            best_exact = float(evaluate_sequence(best_list))
        except Exception:
            best_exact = float("inf")

    total_s = float(sum(best_list))
    if total_s > 0.0 and total_s < 0.01:
        f = 0.01 / total_s
        best_list = [float(x) * f for x in best_list]

    print("FINAL exact score: %.8f" % float(best_exact))
    return best_list
```
